Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the clouds space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 485 and the convergence rate is O(256/n^2).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the clouds decomposition. By minimality, the element a^485 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 256-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)
